# DataBinding paths

Every absolute path under `ScriptDatastoreRoot` that a screen or widget reads, including the ones reached through a binding context. Create the first segment with `DatabindingAddDataContainerFromPath("", root)` and the rest with `DatabindingAddDataContainer`. The type is inferred from what the UI binds the value to: `RawText` takes a string, `Text` a label hash, `Visible` a bool, `Value`/`Minimum`/`Maximum` a number.

> Generated by `tools/gen_docs.py` from the game files. Do not edit by hand.

|---|---|---|---|
